## Sheet: Özet
| The Division 2 — eşya kütüphanesi |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| TD2 Loot Advisor kütüphane sürümü 2026-10-28.10 · Son sezon: Y8S3 Red Horizon |  |  |
| Bölüm | Kayıt | Açıklama |
| Ekipman (tüm) |  | Eşyalar sayfası: her ekipman parçası, yuvası ve markası/seti |
|   High-End parçalar |  |  |
|   Gear Set parçaları |  |  |
|   Named ekipman |  |  |
|   Exotic ekipman |  |  |
| Markalar |  | Bonuslar ve 6 yuvanın parça adları |
| Gear setler |  | Bonuslar, göğüslük/sırt talentleri, parça adları |
| Exoticler |  | Ekipman ve silah, talent ve açıklama |
|   Exotic silah |  |  |
|   Exotic ekipman |  |  |
| Named eşyalar |  | Ekipman ve silah |
| Silahlar |  | Tüm silahlar ve varyantları |
| Ekipman talentleri |  |  |
| Silah talentleri |  |  |
| Ekipman modları |  |  |
| Silah modları |  |  |
| Attribute'lar |  | Programın rulo kalitesini hesapladığı en yüksek değerler |
| Skill'ler |  |  |
| Y8 sezon yenilikleri |  | Rise Up, Into the Dark, Red Horizon |
| Sezon modifiyeleri |  |  |
| Red Horizon değişiklikleri |  |  |
| Red Horizon: Shock Resistance, Health, Incoming Repairs ve Swap Speed marka bonusları kaldırıldı; Protection from Elites eklendi. Yeni marka eklenmeyecek, mevcut markalara named parçalar gelecek. |  |  |
| Red Horizon: Determined artık Perfect Determined gibi çalışıyor; zincirleme gövde→headshot davranışı Iron Will'e taşındı. Steel & Sons ACR: stabilite arttı, en çok yığın 3→4, zayıf nokta yok etme +%30 Amplified Damage (5 sn). |  |  |
| Programa işleme |  |  |
| Eşyalar, Markalar, Gear Setler, Exoticler, Named, Silahlar, talent ve mod sayfalarındaki düzeltme ve eklemeler 'python scripts/import_library_excel.py' ile programın kütüphanesine (meta/game_data.json) işlenir. Satır silmek kütüphaneden silmez. |  |  |
| Kaynaklar |  |  |
| Ubisoft sezon notları: Rise Up, Into the Dark, Red Horizon (ubisoft.com/the-division-2/news-updates) |  |  |
| The Division Wiki — thedivision.fandom.com (CC BY-SA 3.0): marka/set parça adları, exotic bilgileri |  |  |
| mx-division-builds — github.com/mxswat/mx-division-builds (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0): talent açıklamaları, modlar, silah değerleri |  |  |
| keengamer.com, mp1st.com, skycoach.gg: sezon ayrıntıları ve silah türleri |  |  |
| Boş hücre: kaynaklarda bilgi yok (ör. Teapot/Steamer silah türü, Tip of the Spear parça adları). |  |  |

## Sheet: Ekipman Talentleri
| Talent | Yuva | Tür | Açıklama |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Ablative Nano-Plating | Exotic | Exotic | When your or an ally's armor breaks, gain 80% bonus armor for 10s. 45s cooldown. Kills with your specialization weapon resets cooldown. |
| Abridged |  |  | If your Primary and Secondary weapon are not Exotic or Named and of the same type, the Secondary Weapon's talent will also be applied to the Primary Weapon. Doesn't apply to talents that include Weapon Swapping. |
| Accelerando |  |  | Decreases the number of stacks needed to proc the Symphony double buffs from 4 to 3. |
| Ace in the Sleeve |  |  | Amplifies 1 extra shot when revealing your hand. |
| Adrenaline Rush |  |  | When you are within 10m of an enemy, gain 20% bonus armor for 5s. Stacks up to 3 times. Cooldown: 5s |
| Advanced Combat Tactics |  |  | Increases Ground Control proximity repair from 60% to 120%. |
| Afterburn |  |  | Loaded for Bear exotic talent'i. |
| Aggressive Recon |  |  | Getting a Signature Weapon kill gives +15% Signature Weapon Damage for 10s and +25% Reload Speed for the next reload of the weapon (the bonuses do not stack). Automatically regenerate Signature Weapon Ammo every 60s. |
| All for One |  |  | Increase the amount of marks that can be placed from 4 to 8. |
| Alternating Current |  |  | Generate a stack of 2.5% skill damage on one of your skills every second, capping at 10 stacks. After 10 seconds at the cap the stacks transfer to your other skill. The process then repeats ad-infinitum. |
| Apex Predator |  |  | Enemies within 15m receive a debuff, amplifying your weapon damage against them by +20%. Killing a debuffed enemy with your weapon disorients other enemies within 5m, and amplifies weapon damage by 5% for 10s, stacking up to 5 times. |
| Best Buds |  |  | Increase the damage buff from 25% to 50%. |
| Bewildered |  |  | When dealing Weapon Damage to an enemy, 50% of the damage will be dealt to another enemy within 30m instead. When there are no enemies nearby, deal damage normally. Does not apply to Armor Plates. |
| Bleeding Edge |  |  | Shooting enemies within 15m applies bleed to the target. Repair 3-48% of your armor per second for every enemy that is bleeding within 15m. Repair strength per number of bleeding enemies: 1:3% 2:6% 3:12% 4:24% 5:48% |
| Bloodsucker | Sırt çantası | Normal | Killing an enemy adds and refreshes a stack of +10% bonus armor for 10 seconds. Max stack is 10. |
| Bob and Weave |  |  | Receive repairs of 5% of your Total Armor for every 1m run in cover-to-cover. Damage taken while doing a cover-to-cover is healed over 10s. |
| Bond |  |  | Both you and Allies receive +30% Critical Hit Damage, +15% Skill Efficiency and +2% Armor Regen when an Ally is within 10m of you. Receive +15% Critical Hit Damage, +7.5% Skill Efficiency and +1% Armor Regen when one of your Skills is within 10m of you. Only the highest group of buffs can be active at one time. |
| Braced | Göğüslük | Normal | While in cover weapon handling is increased by 45%. |
| Calculated | Sırt çantası | Normal | Kills from cover reduce skill cooldowns by 10%. |
| Camaraderie |  |  | Shooting an enemy marks them for 10 seconds. When a marked enemy dies, receive one stack of 3% Weapon Damage and 3% Critical Hit Damage for each Ally or Skill that has contributed to killing that enemy. Max stacks is 35. Stacks decay every 10 seconds. Maximum amount of marks that can be placed is 4. |
| Chain Combustion |  |  | Enemies set ablaze by the Ortiz Incinerator Turret Prototype ignite other enemies within 10m. |
| Challenger |  |  | Recieve +25% Damage resistance from enemies within 20m. |
| Charging |  |  | For each second spent out of cover during combat, Agents will get 5% reduced incoming skill damage. Max 50%. Charged: While fully charged, gain immunity to any movement speed debuff and share this with all of the agents hazard protection and the incoming skill damage with all allies for 10 seconds. After Charged is consumed, Charging buff will resume if still in combat and out of cover. |
| Chemical Agent |  |  | Catalysis: Dealing and receiving status effects (Burn, Bleed, Shock, EMP/Disrupt, Poison and blind/disorient) builds stacks of Catalysis to a maximum of 12. Each stack grants: +2% Weapon Damage, +2% Status Effects. Each enemy within 25 Meters that is affected by a status effect grants +1 stack/sec. Being affected by status effects grants +2 stacks/sec. Stacks decay at 1 stack/sec after 5s if no enemies in the radius or you are affected by status effects. Reactant: At max stacks, killing a status-affected enemy grants bonuses for 5s: +25% bonus armor. |
| Clutch | Sırt çantası | Normal | If you are below 15% armor, critical hits repair 3% missing armor. Kills allow you to repair up to 100% armor for 4-10 seconds. |
| Cold |  |  | Stacks now supply +2% bonus armor. |
| Combat Medic |  |  | +80% Revive Speed. -50% Weakened Debuff timer. +45% Hive efficiency. Provides +50% damage resistance to both agents while reviving or being revived, and for 5s after a successful Hive Revive, or 10s after a Manual Revive. Any successful revives provide +1 Skill tier for 5s. Revives at skill tier 6 grant Overcharge for 15s. |
| Combined Arms | Sırt çantası | Normal | Shooting an enemy increases total skill damage by 25% for 3 seconds. |
| Companion | Sırt çantası | Normal | While you are within 5 meters of an ally or skill, total weapon damage is increased by 15%. |
| Compiler Optimization | Göğüslük | gear_set | Decreases Hackstep Protocol cooldown from 20 seconds to 15 seconds. |
| Complete Uptime |  |  | Cancelling your skills will reset their cooldown. |
| Composure | Sırt çantası | Normal | Bulldog (Royal Works) talent'i (Perfect). |
| Concussion | Sırt çantası | Normal | Headshots increase weapon damage by 10% for 1.5s. 5s for marksman rifles. Headshot kills increase weapon damage by 15% for 10s. |
| Core Exercise |  |  | Core Strength 4 parça talent'i. |
| Counter |  |  | Swapping weapons will give the following groups of bonuses one by one, in order: 1. +20% Rate of Fire, +20% Weapon Damage. 2. +50% Magazine Size, +50% Reload Speed. The bonuses remain active for 12s or until the next weapon swap. Swapping to your sidearm will not trigger the next group of bonuses. |
| Creeping Death | Sırt çantası | Normal | When you apply a status effect, it is also applied to all enemies within 5 meters of your target. Cooldown is 20 seconds. |
| Critical Measures | Sırt çantası | gear_set | Increases Crowd Control damage to additional marked enemies from 60% to 100%. |
| Crowd Control |  |  | Critical hits mark enemies for 20s, up to 3 marks total. When you critically hit a marked enemy, all other marked enemies take 60% of the damage dealt. Whenever a marked enemy dies, gain +10% critical hit damage, stacking up to 10 times, or until combat ends. |
| Dead Man's Hand |  |  | Flip a card when landing shots with a Rifle or Marksman rifle. After 5 cards are flipped, the damage of your next shot is amplified by 75%. More shots are enhanced the better the hand revealed. Four of a Kind: 4 shots. Full House: 3 shots. Aces and Eights: 2 shots. Flip an additional card on headshots. |
| Deceit |  |  | Enemies targeting your Decoy also count towards the Stoic Damage Reduction bonus. |
| Decoy King |  |  | While in combat, the Moon will cycle through its phases every 5s. There are 8 Moon phases. Starting from a New Moon, weapon damage you deal will be Amplified by 5%. The value of the Amplifier will increase by 5% each phase until reaching 25% during a Full Moon, then it will decrease as the Moon return to being a New Moon, restarting the cycle. During a Full Moon, deploying a Decoy will grant it Overcharge and the cycle will pause for 10s. |
| Defibrillator |  |  | While "Shocker Punch" is equipped, the stun received by the agent from the Shock status effect will be reduced by 50%. Using a shield will give 100% to melee damage. Using St. Elmo's Engine with the holster will give 100% extra melee damage and will make the next melee attack apply shock to the target. Using all three items will offer all of the above mentioned bonuses and the Shock from the melee attack will have a 5m radius, starting from the first target. 15 second cooldown. |
| Dragon's Glare | Exotic | Exotic | While in combat, applies burn to the enemy closest to you within 20 meters. Cooldown: 40 seconds. |
| Efficient | Göğüslük | Normal | Using an armor kit has a 50% chance to not consume the armor kit. Specialization armor kit bonuses are increased by 100%. |
| Emergency Requisition | Sırt çantası | gear_set | Reloading your weapon automatically collects all combat resources (armor kits, ammo, grenades) within 40 meters. |
| Empathic Resolve | Göğüslük | Normal | Benefactor (Edelweiss) talent'i (Perfect). |
| Endless Hunger |  |  | Increases the duration of Apex Predator stacks from 10s to 30s. |
| Energize | Sırt çantası | Normal | Using an armor kit grants +1 skill tier for 15 seconds. If already at skill tier 6, grants overcharge. |
| Entrench | Göğüslük | Normal | If you are below 30% armor, headshots from cover repair 20% of your armor. |
| Escape Plan |  |  | +20% Movement Speed. |
| Explosive Delivery | Göğüslük | Normal | 1.5s after a skill lands, creates an explosion with a 5M radius. Damage scales with skill tier, 25-100% damage of a concussion grenade. |
| Feedback Loop |  |  | Whenever you use or cancel a skill, your other skill's cooldown is reduced by 30s, total skill repair is increased by 10% for 20s, and total skill damage is increased by 10% for 20s. Cooldown: 10s. |
| Field Medic |  |  |  |
| Fireworks Show |  |  | Concussion grenades become Festive Grenades. Hitting an enemy with a Festive Grenade will cause explosions to happen around two other enemies within 20m. 10m in pvp. *Dealing 15% damage in PVP. |
| Flashpoint |  |  | Ember Engine göğüslük: Spontaneous Combustion Burn şansı %60. |
| Flurry |  |  | Status effects apply snowfall to target. |
| Focus | Göğüslük | Normal | Increases total weapon damage by 5% every second you are aiming while scoped 8x or higher, up to 50%. |
| Fortissimo |  |  | Double the weapon damage bonus from Symphony. |
| From the Shadows |  |  | Increases max stacks for Into the Light from 50 to 100, stack gain from 10 to 20 and stack consumption from 10 to 20. |
| From the Shadows Into the Light |  |  | From the Shadows: While out of cover and in combat, gain 10 Stacks per second up to 50. Each stack will give 0.8% Armor regen when it is consumed. Stacks consume, 10 stacks per second, only in cover. Into the Light: While in cover, gain 10 Stacks per second up to 50. Each stack will give 1.2% Critical damage increase and 0.4% RPM. Buff does not apply while shooting from cover. While out of cover Agents lose 2 stacks per second at normal speed and 1 stack per second if sprinting. |
| Galvanize | Sırt çantası | Normal | Applying a Blind, Ensnare, Confuse, or Shock to an enemy grants 40% bonus armor to you and all allies within 20M of the enemy for 10 seconds. |
| Glass Cannon | Göğüslük | Normal | All damage you deal is amplified by 25%. All damage you take is amplified by 50%. |
| Grace Under Fire | Exotic | Exotic | Cannot be staggered by explosions. Staying in cover without firing for 6 - 12s provides damage immunity while aiming for 1 - 2s. |
| Ground Control |  |  | Increases you and your allies' total weapon and skill damage by 15% when at full armor. When you repair an ally, you and all allies within 5m of you are also repaired for 60% of that amount. |
| Gunslinger | Göğüslük | Normal | Robin (Royal Works) talent'i (Perfect). |
| Hackstep Protocol |  |  | Replaces armor kits with an instant, infinite-use ability. Grants 50% bonus armor for 5s. Hides your nameplate for 5s. Repairs 20% of your armor. Increases total weapon damage by 2% per 5% bonus armor gained, up to 20%. Ability cooldown: 20s. |
| Headache |  |  | First Headshot with a Marksman Rifle will increase next headshot by 80%, second consecutive headshot with a Marksman Rifle will give +10% armor (if at full armor it will give bonus armor max +50% of current armor value), third consecutive headshot will refill magazine. From the fourth headshot forward agents will get all 3 bonuses for each consecutive headshot kill. Missing a headshot will reset the cycle. |
| Headhunter | Göğüslük | Normal | After killing an enemy with a headshot, your next weapon hit within 30s deals 125% of that killing blow's damage in addition to it. Damage is capped to 800% of your weapon damage. This is raised to 1250% if your headshot damage is greater than 150%. |
| Heartstopper |  |  | Headshots apply pulse for 5s. Weapon hits on pulsed enemies add and refresh a stack of +1% bonus armor and +1.1% amplified weapon damage to pulsed enemies for 5s. Max stack is 50. Two stacks are lost per second. |
| Heatstroke |  |  | +40% Amplified Weapon Damage to enemies set on fire by the Ortiz Incinerator Turret Prototype. +25% Ortiz Incinerator Turret Prototype Range. |
| Hivemind |  |  | Decrease the Overcharge cooldown from 40s to 25s. |
